MERIT MUR human audit — 100 episodes

For each item: read the memory block and the transcript, then judge whether the agent's executed tool calls made use of the gold value(s) it needed from memory (verbatim or reformatted — e.g. units or formatting changes still count as use; the value merely appearing in the user's words or in memory does not). Record yes, no, or unclear per item in labels.csv. Do not consult key.csv until both annotators are done.
